-- =====================================================================
-- Urban Plate Kitchens: sample restaurant-chain data warehouse
-- Star schema for Microsoft Fabric Warehouse / Fabric data agent demos
-- All names, people and figures are fictional, generated sample data.
-- Currency: USD.  Fiscal year starts 1 April (FY2025 = Apr 2024 - Mar 2025).
-- Date range: 1 Apr 2023 - 31 Mar 2025 (two full fiscal years).
-- =====================================================================

IF EXISTS (
    SELECT * FROM sys.sysobjects
    WHERE name = 'DimRestaurant')
DROP TABLE dbo.DimRestaurant;
GO

CREATE TABLE dbo.DimRestaurant
(
    RestaurantKey INT NOT NULL,
    RestaurantAltKey VARCHAR(20) NULL,
    RestaurantName VARCHAR(60) NOT NULL,
    RestaurantFormat VARCHAR(20) NULL,
    City VARCHAR(50) NULL,
    StateProvince VARCHAR(50) NULL,
    SalesRegion VARCHAR(20) NULL,
    CountryRegion VARCHAR(50) NULL,
    OpenDate DATE NULL,
    SeatingCapacity INT NULL
);
GO

IF EXISTS (
    SELECT * FROM sys.sysobjects
    WHERE name = 'DimMenuItem')
DROP TABLE dbo.DimMenuItem;
GO

CREATE TABLE dbo.DimMenuItem
(
    MenuItemKey INT NOT NULL,
    MenuItemAltKey VARCHAR(20) NULL,
    MenuItemName VARCHAR(60) NOT NULL,
    Category VARCHAR(30) NULL,
    IsVegetarian BIT NULL,
    IsSpicy BIT NULL,
    ListPrice DECIMAL(10,2) NULL,
    FoodCost DECIMAL(10,2) NULL
);
GO

IF EXISTS (
    SELECT * FROM sys.sysobjects
    WHERE name = 'DimChannel')
DROP TABLE dbo.DimChannel;
GO

CREATE TABLE dbo.DimChannel
(
    ChannelKey INT NOT NULL,
    ChannelName VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
    ChannelDescription VARCHAR(60) NULL
);
GO

GO

IF EXISTS (
    SELECT * FROM sys.sysobjects
    WHERE name = 'DimDaypart')
DROP TABLE dbo.DimDaypart;
GO

CREATE TABLE dbo.DimDaypart
(
    DaypartKey INT NOT NULL,
    DaypartName VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    StartHour INT NULL,
    EndHour INT NULL
);
GO

IF EXISTS (
    SELECT * FROM sys.sysobjects
    WHERE name = 'DimCustomer')
DROP TABLE dbo.DimCustomer;
GO

CREATE TABLE dbo.DimCustomer
(
    CustomerKey INT NOT NULL,
    CustomerAltKey VARCHAR(80) NULL,
    FirstName VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    LastName VARCHAR(50) NULL,
    HomeCity VARCHAR(50) NULL,
    StateProvince VARCHAR(50) NULL,
    LoyaltyTier VARCHAR(20) NULL,
    JoinDate DATE NULL
);
GO

IF EXISTS (
    SELECT * FROM sys.sysobjects
    WHERE name = 'DimDate')
DROP TABLE dbo.DimDate;
GO

CREATE TABLE dbo.DimDate
(
    DateKey INT NOT NULL,
    DateAltKey DATE NOT NULL,
    DayOfWeek INT NOT NULL,
    WeekDayName VARCHAR(10),
    DayOfMonth INT NOT NULL,
    [Month] INT NOT NULL,
    MonthName VARCHAR(12),
    [Year] INT NOT NULL,
    [Quarter] INT NOT NULL,
    FiscalYear INT NOT NULL,
    FiscalQuarter INT NOT NULL,
    IsWeekend BIT NOT NULL
);
GO

(
    OrderLineKey INT NOT NULL,

);
